# ==== Crown_k0.txt ====
name = 'Crown_k0'
    smallness_parameters = ['t1']
    internal_lines = [['0', [5, 1]], ['0', [5, 2]], ['0', [5, 3]], ['0', [5, 4]], ['0', [6, 1]], ['0', [6, 2]], ['0', [6, 3]], ['0', [6, 4]]]
    external_lines = [['p1', 1], ['p2', 2], ['p3', 3], ['p4', 4]]
    li = psd.LoopIntegralFromGraph(
       internal_lines = internal_lines,
       external_lines = external_lines,
       replacement_rules = [('p1*p1', 'm1**2'), ('p2*p2', 'm2**2'), ('p3*p3', 'm3**2'), ('p4*p4', 'm4**2'), ('p1*p2', '(s12-m1**2+m2**2)/2'), ('p1*p3', '(s13-m1**2+m3**2)/2'), ('p1*p4', '(s14-m1**2+m4**2)/2'), ('m1**2', 't1*m1s'), ('m2**2', 't1*m2s'), ('m3**2', 't1*m3s'), ('m4**2', 't1*m4s')]
    )

    internal_lines = [[0, [1, 5]], [0, [1, 11]], [0, [2, 8]], [0, [2, 9]], [0, [3, 10]], [0, [3, 12]], [0, [4, 6]], [0, [4, 7]], [0, [5, 6]], [0, [7, 8]], [0, [9, 10]], [0, [5, 12]], [0, [6, 9]], [0, [7, 12]], [0, [8, 11]], [0, [10, 11]]]
    external_lines = [['p1', 1], ['p2', 2], ['p3', 3], ['p4', 4]]
    li = psd.LoopIntegralFromGraph(
       internal_lines = internal_lines,
       external_lines = external_lines,
       replacement_rules = [
                              ('p1*p1', 'm1**2'),
                              ('p2*p2', 'm2**2'),
                              ('p3*p3', 'm3**2'),
                              ('p4*p4', 'm4**2'),
                              ('p1*p2', '(s12-m1**2+m2**2)/2'),
                              ('p1*p3', '(s13-m1**2+m3**2)/2'),
                              ('p1*p4', '(s14-m1**2+m4**2)/2'),
                              ('m1**2', 't1*m1s'),
                              ('m2**2', 't1*m2s'),
                              ('m3**2', '0'),
                              ('m4**2', '0'),
                              ]
    )
